- **textedit.es**: Cómo funciona EDITARTEXTO (TEXTEDIT, alias ED) de AutoCAD en español: editar textos, textos de líneas múltiples y atributos, uno tras otro. Pruébalo gratis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.es.md))
- **textedit.fr**: Comment MODIFTEXTE (TEXTEDIT, alias ED) d'AutoCAD modifie textes, textes multilignes et définitions d'attributs, l'un après l'autre, avec Mode. Essayez-le. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.fr.md))
- **textedit.hu**: Így szerkeszt az AutoCAD SZÖVSZERK (TEXTEDIT) parancsa szöveget, bekezdéses szöveget és attribútumdefiníciót egymás után, Vissza és Mód opcióval. Próbálja ki! ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.hu.md))
- **textedit.it**: Come MODTESTO (TEXTEDIT, alias MOD, DDEDIT) di AutoCAD modifica testo, testo multilinea e definizioni di attributo, uno dopo l'altro. Provalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.it.md))
- **textedit.ja**: AutoCAD の TEXTEDIT[文字編集](ED)で文字、マルチ テキスト、属性定義を続けて修正する方法を解説。元に戻すとモード。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.ja.md))
- **textedit.ko**: AutoCAD TEXTEDIT(문자 편집, ED)로 문자, 여러 줄 문자, 속성 정의를 차례로 편집하는 방법과 명령 취소, 모드 옵션을 설명합니다. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.ko.md))
- **textedit.pl**: Jak polecenie EDTEKST (TEXTEDIT, skrót EDT) w AutoCADzie edytuje tekst, tekst wielowierszowy i definicje atrybutów, jeden po drugim, z Cofaj i Tryb. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.pl.md))
- **textedit.pt-BR**: Como o TEXTEDIT do AutoCAD em português, EDITARTEXTO (alias ED), edita textos, textos multilinha e definições de atributo, um após o outro. Experimente grátis. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.pt-BR.md))
- **textedit.ru**: Как ТЕКСТРЕД (TEXTEDIT, псевдоним РЕД) в AutoCAD редактирует текст, многострочный текст и определения атрибутов, один за другим. Попробуйте бесплатно. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.ru.md))
- **textedit.zh-CN**: AutoCAD TEXTEDIT（编辑文字，快捷键 ED、DDEDIT）怎么用：逐个编辑单行文字、多行文字和属性定义，以及放弃和模式选项。在浏览器中免费试用。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/textedit.zh-CN.md))
- **textless**: textless on or off in this chat, change the scene set, open settings or your scenes folder. /textless on, off, settings, set, next, get, folder, update, or nothing to toggle. ([HeyCubit/effortless](https://github.com/HeyCubit/effortless/blob/HEAD/textless/plugin/commands/textless.md) · ★352 · 3 copies)
- **textlint**: Execute textlint on specified files, apply automatic fixes, manually fix remaining errors, and repeat until all errors are resolved ([ohtaman/mip-mcp](https://github.com/ohtaman/mip-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/textlint.md) · ★2 · 3 copies)
- **textme**: Enable "text me" mode. When enabled, Claude will automatically text you via iMessage when: - A task is completed - Claude needs your input or approval - Claude is blocked and needs guidance ([njerschow/textme](https://github.com/njerschow/textme/blob/HEAD/commands/textme.md) · ★92)
- **texto-teleprompter**: Adapta un guion para leerlo a cámara con marcas de pausa y énfasis, y genera un teleprompter en HTML ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/video-guion/commands/texto-teleprompter.md))
- **textos-legales-web**: Redacta el borrador de aviso legal, política de privacidad y política de cookies para una web publicada en España. Se activa cuando el usuario pide textos legales para su web o dice que va a publicarla y no tiene nada. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-alternativas/commands/textos-legales-web.md))
- **textos-legales-web-2**: Redacta el aviso legal, la política de privacidad y la política de cookies de una web española a partir de los datos del negocio. Úsalo cuando publiques una web o landing nueva, o cuando un cliente te pase una sin textos legales. ([Nawii99/arsenal-piezas](https://github.com/Nawii99/arsenal-piezas/blob/HEAD/plugins/web-alternativas/commands/textos-legales-web-2.md))
- **texts-list**: `texts list` reads Text records from the project's current Baseline and returns their GUIDs, titles, and interlinearization data. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/texts-list.md) · ★1)
- **texttomd**: 适用于你要把推文、微博这种纯文本内容转成格式良好的博客，可以帮你生成标题、列表，和加粗要点、金句。 ([zsutxz/ClaudeLearning](https://github.com/zsutxz/ClaudeLearning/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/texttomd.md) · ★5)
- **textual-debug**: Debug and troubleshoot Textual applications with comprehensive diagnostic tools, error tracking, and performance analysis. ([Jrakru/marketplace_UI](https://github.com/Jrakru/marketplace_UI/blob/HEAD/textual-plugin/commands/textual-debug.md))
- **textual-init**: Initialize a new Textual project with a complete, production-ready template structure and configuration. ([Jrakru/marketplace_UI](https://github.com/Jrakru/marketplace_UI/blob/HEAD/textual-plugin/commands/textual-init.md))
- **textual-new-widget**: Create a new custom Textual widget with proper structure, styling, events, and documentation following best practices. ([Jrakru/marketplace_UI](https://github.com/Jrakru/marketplace_UI/blob/HEAD/textual-plugin/commands/textual-new-widget.md))
- **textual-run**: Run Textual applications with debug options, profiling, testing modes, and development features. ([Jrakru/marketplace_UI](https://github.com/Jrakru/marketplace_UI/blob/HEAD/textual-plugin/commands/textual-run.md))
- **textual-style**: Apply Textual styling, themes, CSS utilities, and design patterns to create beautiful and consistent TUI applications. ([Jrakru/marketplace_UI](https://github.com/Jrakru/marketplace_UI/blob/HEAD/textual-plugin/commands/textual-style.md))
